```
def _score_seq(a):
    a=np.asarray(a); n=len; m=pow2(2n-1); F=fft(a,m); C=ifft(F*F).real[:2n-1]; bmax=max; return 2n*bmax/(sum a)^2 (sum? if normalized)
```

Score+weights:
```
def _smooth_score_grad_a(a, tau):
    n, m, F, C
    cmax=C.max()
    x=(C-cmax)/tau
    x=clip(x,-700,700)
    S=np.exp(x).sum()
    if S not finite: alpha uniform active? 
    alpha=exp(x)/S
    J=cmax+tau*math.log(S)
    P=fft(alpha,m)
    g=2*ifft(P*conj(F)).real[:n]
    # ensure g finite
    return J, alpha, g
```
But if tau small and cmax large? C O(1/n). tau absolute. If tau <=0, use top uniform.

```
def _spectral_sqrt(start_a, target, n, m, iters, blend=0.5, score_every=10):
    target = np.maximum(target,0); target /= target.sum() maybe
    T = np.fft.fft(target, m)
    a = np.maximum(start_a,1e-12); a=a/a.sum()
    # initialize a to sqrt(T) inverse positive:
    sqrt_T=np.fft.ifft(np.sqrt(T)).real[:n]; sp=np.maximum(sqrt_T,0); if sum>0 sp/sp.sum()
    a = 0.5*a+0.5*sp
    best=sc...
    for it:
       F=fft(a,m)
       Fsafe = F + eps if needed
       Fnew=0.5*(F + T/Fsafe)
       anew=ifft(Fnew).real[:n]
       pos=maximum(anew); if sum small pos=a
       bld = max(0.2, blend*(1-0.7*it/iters))
       a_new=(1-bld)*a+bld*pos; a_new=max(a_new); normalize
       score; update best
    return best
```

```
      except Exception as e: print; break
      if res.x finite:

         if sc<best_s-1e-12: best_s,best_a=a,no_improve=0 else no_improve+1 print

   # final convert best
```

`_score_fft` for a normalized? We'll use sum general:
```
def _score_fft(a):
  a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
  n=len(a)
  if n==0: return inf
  s=a.sum(); if s<=0 return inf
  m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
  F=fft(a,m)
  C=ifft(F*F).real[:2*n-1]
  C=np.maximum(C,0)
  bm=C.max()
  if bm<=0 or not finite: return inf
  return float(2.0*n*bm/(s*s))
```

Now, code specifics.

`_softmax`:
```
def _softmax(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64)
    v=v-np.max(v)
    v=np.clip(v,-745.0,709.0)
    e=np.exp(v)
    s=e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
        return np.full(v.shape, 1.0/v.size)
    return e/s
```

`_v_from_x`:
```
def _v_from_x(x,n):
    v=np.empty(n,dtype=np.float64)
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size != n-1: x=np.resize(x,n-1)
    v[:-1]=x
    s=float(np.sum(x))
    v[-1]= -s/float(n)
    return v
```
If x contains large, sum could overflow? x clipped in L-BFGS. Perturb clip.

`_a_from_x` simply `_softmax(_v_from_x(x,n))`.

`_x_from_a`:
```
def _x_from_a(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if a.size != n: a=_interp(a,n)
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-300)
   s=a.sum(); if s<=0: a=np.ones(n); s=n
   a=a/s
   v=np.log(a)
   v-=float(np.mean(v))
   return v[:-1].copy()
```

`_score_grad_smooth` function with scale:
```
def _score_grad_smooth(a, tau, m):
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1; scale=2.0*n
    F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
    C=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:L]
    C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
    cmax=float(C.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax<=0: return (large, None)
    if tau <= 1e-12:
       active = C >= cmax - 1e-12*max(1,cmax)
       if not active.any(): active[0]=True? cmax index
       alpha=active.astype(float); alpha/=alpha.sum()
       J=scale*cmax
    else:
       x=(C-cmax)/tau; x=np.clip(x,-745,709); ex=np.exp(x); S=ex.sum()
       if not finite S: active max; alpha=...
       else alpha=ex/S; J=scale*(cmax + tau*math.log(S))
    P=np.fft.fft(alpha,m)
    g=scale*2.0*np.fft.ifft(P*np.conj(F)).real[:n]
    # Clip g for finiteness
    if not all finite: g=np.zeros(n)
    return J,g
```
Need `math.log`; import math. `np.all(np.isfinite(g))` maybe O(n) cost but okay. Could just `if not np.isfinite(g.sum())`.

For tau small and active many? okay.

Objective `_obj_x` calls `_score_grad_smooth(a,tau,m)[0]`. If N/A return 1e6.
`_grad_x` similarly returns `g_v[:-1]`:
```
def _grad_x(x,n,tau,m):
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return np.zeros(n-1)
   v=_v_from_x(x,n); if not finite v: zeros
   a=_softmax(v)
   J,g=_score_grad_smooth(a,tau,m)
   if not finite J or g is None: return zeros
   mu=float(np.dot(a,g))
   gv=a*(g-mu)
   if not np.isfinite(gv.sum()): return zeros
   out=gv[:-1].copy()
   # clip? L-BFGS can handle; maybe clip to 10
   return out
```
No need clip.

`_polish` implementation details:
```
def _polish(a, n, budget, rng):
    a=_prep_for_search(a,n)
    if a is None: return None,inf
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_score_fft(a)
    # apply local simple first
    a,sc=_local_simple(a,n); if sc<best_score: update
    x=_x_from_a(a,n)
    m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    bounds=...
    fracs=[1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03,0.015,0.0075,0.00375]
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(1.0,budget)
    no_improve=0; stage=0; perturb_count=0
    maxfun = ...
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
       if stage < len(fracs): fr=fracs[stage]
       else: fr = max(0.001, fracs[-1]*(0.75**(stage-len(fracs))))
       cmax=_bmax(a)
       if not finite cmax or cmax<=0: break
       tau = max(1e-9, fr*cmax)
       opts = {'maxiter': maxfun, 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-12, 'disp':False}
       if _sciopt is not None:
          try:
             res=_sciopt.minimize(_obj_x, x, jac=_grad_x, args=(n,tau,m), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=opts)
             if np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)): x=res.x
          except Exception as e:
             print(" lbfq fail", e)
       else:
          x = _adam_global(x,n,tau,m, min(3.0, max(0.5, budget*0.05)), rng) # fallback
       a=_a_from_x(x,n)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)) or a.sum()<=0: break
       sc=_score_fft(a)
       improved=False
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy(); improved=True; no_improve=0
           print(" polish n=%d stage=%d score=%.8f"...)
       else:
           no_improve += 1
       a, sc2 = _local_simple(a,n)
       if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=sc2; best_a=a.copy()
           if not improved: no_improve=0? but local improved; reset? yes.
       x=_x_from_a(a,n)
       if no_improve >= 4:
           sigma = 0.03 + 0.05 * np.sqrt(no_improve)
           x = x + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n-1)
           x = np.clip(x, -80, 80)
           no_improve = 0
           perturb_count +=1
           if perturb_count > 15: break
       stage += 1
       if stage > 100: break
    # final local on best_a?
    best_a, bs = _local_simple(best_a,n)
    if bs < best_score: best_score=bs
    return best_a,best_score
```

```
def _local_simple(a,n):
   a=_prep_for_search(a,n)
   best=a; best_s=_score_fft(a)
   def tryb? no nested. Top-level can't. We'll inline loop:
   cands=[]
```

```
def _select_candidates(scored, max_keep=6):
  scored.sort(key=_pair_score)
  kept=[]
  for sc,a in scored:
    if not finite: continue
    add=True
    for _,b in kept:
       if np.sqrt(np.sum((a-b)**2)) < 0.02: add=False; break
    if add: kept.append((sc,a)); if len(kept)>=max_keep: break
  return kept
```

Let's fill helper functions:

`_next_pow2`:
```
def _next_pow2(x):
    x=int(max(1,x)); p=1
    while p<x: p <<=1
    return p
```

`_interp` as before.

`_prep`:
```
def _prep_sequence(seq, n=None):
    arr=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    if n is not None and arr.size != n: arr=_interp(arr,n)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(arr.size)/arr.size if arr.size>0 else None
    return arr/s
```

```
def _spectral_solver(start, target, n, m, iters, blend, score_every):
    target = np.asarray(target,float).ravel()
    L=2*n-1
    t=np.zeros(L); t[:min(len(target),L)]=target[:L]; t=np.maximum(t,0); s=t.sum(); if s<=0: t=np.ones(L); s=L
    t=t/s
    T=np.fft.fft(t,m)
    a=np.asarray(start,float); a=np.maximum(a,1e-14); s2=a.sum(); if s2<=0: ones; else a=a/s2
    # init from sqrt
    sp=np.fft.ifft(np.sqrt(T)).real[:n]; sp=np.maximum(sp,0); sps=sp.sum()
    if sps>0 and finite: sp=sp/sps else sp=a.copy()
    a=0.6*a+0.4*sp; a=np.maximum(a,1e-14); a=a/a.sum()
    best=a; best_sc=_score_fft(a)
    Fabs_max? Need eps.
    for it in range(iters):
       F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
       eps=1e-10*(np.max(np.abs(F))+1.0)
       Fnew=0.5*(F + T/(F+eps))
       anew=np.fft.ifft(Fnew).real[:n]
       pos=np.maximum(anew,0); ps=pos.sum()
       if ps < 1e-8: pos=a.copy()
       bld = max(0.2, blend*(1.0 - 0.6*it/iters))
       an=(1-bld)*a + bld*pos
       an=np.maximum(an,1e-14); an=an/an.sum()
       a=an
       if (it+1)%score_every==0:
          sc=_score_fft(a); if sc<best_sc: best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
    sc=_score_fft(a); if sc<best_sc: best=a
    return _prep_sequence(best), float(best_sc)
```

```
def _stage_search(n, budget, parent_seed, rng):
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(30.0, float(budget))
    cap = 450 if n <= 4096 else 250
    cands=_make_candidates(n,rng,parent_seed,cap)
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 60.0 and n <= 8192:
       spec_budget = min(90.0, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.2)
       spec=_spectral_starts(n,parent_seed,spec_budget)
       for a,sc in spec: _add_candidate(cands,a)
    max_keep = 5 if n<=2048 else (4 if n<=4096 else 3)
    top=_select_top(cands, max_keep)
    if not top:
       ones=np.ones(n); top=[(_score_fft(ones), ones)]
    best_a=top[0][1].copy(); best_s=top[0][0]
    weights=[0.45,0.25,0.15,0.10,0.05]
    weights=weights[:len(top)]
    remw=sum(weights)
    for idx, (s, a) in enumerate(top):
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem < 15.0: break
       w=weights[idx]
       alloc = rem * (w / remw) if remw > 0 else rem
       if idx == len(top)-1:
           alloc = max(10.0, rem-5.0)
       else:
           alloc = max(10.0, alloc)
       # don't exceed rem-5
       alloc = min(alloc, rem-5.0)
       if alloc < 8.0: break
       print(" stage n=%d opt cand %d init=%.8f budget=%.1f" % (n,idx,s,alloc))
       aa,ss = _polish(a,n,alloc,rng)
       if aa is not None and np.isfinite(ss) and ss < best_s:
           best_s=ss; best_a=aa.copy()
       remw -= w
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 25.0:
       # polish best again but cap if high n
       extra = min(300.0, rem-5.0) if n<=4096 else min(120.0, rem-5.0)
       if extra > 10.0:
          print(...)
          aa,ss=_polish(best_a,n,extra,rng)
          if finite and ss<best_s: update
    return best_a,float(best_s)
```

```
def _add_unique(pool, a, thr):
   b=_prep_sequence(a)
   if b is None: return
```

```
def _adam_polish(a,n,budget,rng):
    a,bs=_local_simple(a,n)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=bs
    x=_x_from_a(a,n)
    m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    mu=np.zeros(n-1); nu=np.zeros(n-1); t=0
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(1.0,budget); last_improve=start
    while time < deadline-5:
       progress=...
       cmax=_bmax(a); if not finite break
       fr=0.25*(1.0-progress)+0.01
       tau=max(1e-9, fr*cmax)
       v=_v_from_x(x,n); if not finite: perturb; continue
       aa=_softmax(v)
       J,g=_score_grad_smooth(aa,tau,m)
       if not finite: break
       md=float(np.dot(aa,g)); gv=aa*(g-md); gx=gv[:-1]
       if not finite gx: perturb
       t+=1
       mu=0.9mu+0.1gx; nu=0.999nu+0.001gx*gx
       b1=0.9**t; b2=0.999**t; mh=mu/(1-b1); vh=nu/(1-b2)
       lr=0.3*(1-0.7*progress)+0.02
       step=mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+1e-8); step=clip(step,-0.25,0.25)
       x=x-lr*step; clip
       if t%10==0:
          newa=_a_from_x(x,n); newa,sc=_local_simple(newa,n)
          if finite and sc<best_score: update last_improve
          else a=newa maybe? We should set a=newa to continue from current x even if no improve.
       if time-last>25:
          x += rng.normal(0,0.05+0.05*progress,n-1); last=time
       a=_a_from_x(x,n)
    return best_a,best_score
```

Detailed functions code:
```
def _score_grad_smooth(a, tau, m):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    scale = 2.0 * float(n)
    F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
    C = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
    C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
    cmax = float(C.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return 1e18, None
    if tau <= 1e-12:
        active = C >= max(cmax - 1e-12 * max(1.0, cmax), 0.0)
        if not active.any():
            active[np.argmax(C)] = True
        alpha = active.astype(np.float64)
        s = float(alpha.sum())
        if s <= 0.0: return 1e18, None
        alpha /= s
        J = scale * cmax
    else:
        z = (C - cmax) / tau
        z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 709.0)
        e = np.exp(z)
        S = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
            active = C >= cmax - 1e-10 * max(1.0, cmax)
            if not active.any(): active[np.argmax(C)] = True
            alpha = active.astype(np.float64); s=...; alpha/=s
            J = scale * cmax
        else:
            alpha = e / S
            J = scale * (cmax + tau * math.log(S))
    P = np.fft.fft(alpha, m)
    g = scale * 2.0 * np.fft.ifft(P * np.conj(F)).real[:n]
    if not np.isfinite(float(np.sum(g))):
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(J), g
```
`active[np.argmax(C)] = True` if active is bool array; argmax on C maybe. Good.

In tau small active threshold uses `max(cmax - 1e-12*max(1,cmax), 0)`. For cmax small 1e-4, threshold 1e-14. Good.

`_obj_x`:
```
def _obj_x(x,n,tau,m):
   v=_v_from_x(x,n)
   if not np.isfinite(v.sum()): return 1e18
   a=_softmax(v)
   J,g=_score_grad_smooth(a,tau,m)
   if not np.isfinite(J) or g is None: return 1e18
   return J
```
`_grad_x` similarly.

Potential inefficiency: `_obj_x` and `_grad_x` each compute C and F. L-BFGS calls both; okay.

`_v_from_x`:
```
def _v_from_x(x,n):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != n-1:
       x=np.resize(x,n-1)
    s=float(np.sum(x))
    if not np.isfinite(s): return np.zeros(n)
    shift=s/float(n)
    v=np.empty(n,dtype=np.float64)
    v[:-1]=x-shift
    v[-1]=-shift
    return v
```
If x huge sum inf? clip before. Good.

`_x_from_a`:
```
def _x_from_a(a,n):
   b=_prep_sequence(a,n)
   if b is None: return np.zeros(max(1,n-1))
   amax=float(b.max())
   if amax <=0: return zeros
   floor = max(1e-300, amax*1e-18)
   bb=np.maximum(b,floor)
   bb=bb/bb.sum()
   logb=np.log(bb)
   x=logb[:-1]-logb[-1]
   if not np.isfinite(x.sum()): x=np.zeros(n-1)
   return np.clip(x,-100.0,100.0)
```

```
    m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    L=2*n-1
    targets=[]

       targets.append(1.0 + eb*np.power(edge,1.5))
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequence heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

This implementation combines:
  - robust analytic initial shapes, especially endpoint singularities;
  - a small spectral square-root constructor for selected target convolutions;
  - path-following L-BFGS-B optimization of a smooth max(tsau) surrogate in a
    log-ratio parameterization;
  - local smoothing / mirror-symmetry checks;
  - an Adam fallback if scipy L-BFGS-B is unavailable.

The best sequence found before the time deadline is returned.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _sciopt
except Exception:
    _sciopt = None


def _pair_score(pair):
    return pair[0]


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    p = 1
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _interp(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0 or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(int(max(1, n)), dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == int(n):
        return a / s
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a / s)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    ss = float(out.sum())
    if ss <= 0.0:
        out.fill(1.0 / int(n))
    else:
        out /= ss
    return out


def _prep_sequence(seq, n=None):
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    if n is not None and arr.size != int(n):
        arr = _interp(arr, int(n))
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        if arr.size == 0:
            return None
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def _softmax(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = v - float(np.max(v))
    v = np.clip(v, -745.0, 709.0)
    e = np.exp(v)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(v.shape, 1.0 / max(1, v.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _v_from_x(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != n - 1:
        x = np.resize(x, n - 1)
    sx = float(np.sum(x))
    if not np.isfinite(sx):
        return np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    shift = sx / float(n)
    v = np.empty(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    v[:-1] = x - shift
    v[-1] = -shift
    return v


def _a_from_x(x, n):
    return _softmax(_v_from_x(x, n))


def _x_from_a(a, n):
    b = _prep_sequence(a, n)
    if b is None:
        return np.zeros(max(1, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    amax = float(b.max())
    if amax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(amax):
        b = np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        floor = max(1e-300, amax * 1e-18)
        b = np.maximum(b, floor)
        b = b / float(b.sum())
    logb = np.log(b)
    x = logb[:-1] - logb[-1]
    if not np.isfinite(float(np.sum(x))):
        x = np.zeros(max(1, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    return np.clip(x, -100.0, 100.0)


def _score_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
    C = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:2 * n - 1]
    C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
    bm = float(C.max())
    if not np.isfinite(bm) or bm <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * bm / (s * s))


def _bmax(a):
    b = _prep_sequence(a)
    if b is None:
        return np.inf
    n = int(b.size)
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    F = np.fft.fft(b, m)
    C = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:2 * n - 1]
    C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
    cm = float(C.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return cm


def _conv_coeffs(a):
    b = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(b.size)
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    F = np.fft.fft(b, m)
    C = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:2 * n - 1]
    return np.maximum(C, 0.0)


def _score_grad_smooth(a, tau, m):
    """Smooth scaled objective 2n*softmax(C) and its gradient wrt a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    scale = 2.0 * float(n)

    F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
    C = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
    C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
    cmax = float(C.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return 1e18, None

    if tau <= 1e-12:
        thr = max(0.0, cmax - 1e-12 * max(1.0, cmax))
        active = C >= thr
        if not active.any():
            active[int(np.argmax(C))] = True
        alpha = active.astype(np.float64)
        s = float(alpha.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return 1e18, None
        alpha /= s
        J = scale * cmax
    else:
        z = (C - cmax) / tau
        z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 709.0)
        e = np.exp(z)
        S = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
            thr = max(0.0, cmax - 1e-10 * max(1.0, cmax))
            active = C >= thr
            if not active.any():
                active[int(np.argmax(C))] = True
            alpha = active.astype(np.float64)
            s = float(alpha.sum())
            if s <= 0.0:
                return 1e18, None
            alpha /= s
            J = scale * cmax
        else:
            alpha = e / S
            J = scale * (cmax + tau * math.log(S))

    P = np.fft.fft(alpha, m)
    g = scale * 2.0 * np.fft.ifft(P * np.conj(F)).real[:n]
    if not np.isfinite(float(np.sum(g))):
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(J), g


def _obj_x(x, n, tau, m):
    v = _v_from_x(x, n)
    if not np.isfinite(float(np.sum(v))):
        return 1e18
    a = _softmax(v)
    g = _score_grad_smooth(a, tau, m)
    if not np.isfinite(g[0]) or g[1] is None:
        return 1e18
    return g[0]


def _grad_x(x, n, tau, m):
    n = int(n)
    zero = np.zeros(max(1, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != n - 1:
        x = np.resize(x, n - 1)
    if not np.isfinite(float(np.sum(x))):
        return zero
    v = _v_from_x(x, n)
    if not np.isfinite(float(np.sum(v))):
        return zero
    a = _softmax(v)
    g = _score_grad_smooth(a, tau, m)
    if not np.isfinite(g[0]) or g[1] is None:
        return zero
    g_a = g[1]
    mu = float(np.dot(a, g_a))
    gv = a * (g_a - mu)
    if not np.isfinite(float(np.sum(gv))):
        return zero
    out = gv[:-1].copy()
    if not np.isfinite(float(np.sum(out))):
        return zero
    return out


def _add_candidate(cands, a):
    b = _prep_sequence(a)
    if b is not None and b.size > 0:
        cands.append(b)


def _make_candidates(n, rng, parent, cap=350):
    n = int(max(8, n))
    cands = []
    limit = int(max(50, cap))

    if parent is not None:
        p = _prep_sequence(parent, n)
        if p is not None:
            _add_candidate(cands, p)
            _add_candidate(cands, p[::-1])
            _add_candidate(cands, 0.5 * (p + p[::-1]))

    _add_candidate(cands, np.ones(n))

    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = i / float(max(1, n - 1))

    glist = [0.3, 0.5, 0.7]
    lefts = []
    rights = []
    for gg in glist:
        lefts.append(np.power(i + 1.0, -gg))
        rights.append(np.power(n - i, -gg))

    wfr = [0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0]
    bas = [0.0, 0.05, 0.2, 0.8]

    for L in lefts:
        if len(cands) >= limit:
            break
        for R in rights:
            if len(cands) >= limit:
                break
            for wf in wfr:
                if len(cands) >= limit:
                    break
                E = wf * L + (1.0 - wf) * R
                for bb in bas:
                    if len(cands) >= limit:
                        break
                    if bb > 0.0:
                        _add_candidate(cands, E + bb)
                    else:
                        _add_candidate(cands, E)

    for _ in range(100):
        if len(cands) >= limit + 70:
            break
        gl = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        gr = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        L = np.power(i + 1.0, -gl)
        R = np.power(n - i, -gr)
        wf = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
        bb = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.5))
        E = wf * L + (1.0 - wf) * R
        if bb > 0.0:
            _add_candidate(cands, E + bb)
        else:
            _add_candidate(cands, E)

    for _ in range(40):
        if len(cands) >= limit + 120:
            break
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        kc = min(8, n)
        for k in range(1, kc + 1):
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35)) / (float(k) ** 0.7)
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
            a = a + amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * float(k) * x + ph)
        _add_candidate(cands, a)

    if parent is not None:
        p = _prep_sequence(parent, n)
        if p is not None:
            lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
            for amp in (0.01, 0.03, 0.08, 0.18):
                v = lp + rng.normal(0.0, amp, n)
                e = np.exp(v - float(np.max(v)))
                s = float(e.sum())
                if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
                    _add_candidate(cands, e / s)

    return cands


def _select_top(cands, max_keep=5):
    scored = []
    for a in cands:
        b = _prep_sequence(a)
        if b is None:
            continue
        sc = _score_fft(b)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, b))
    scored.sort(key=_pair_score)
    kept = []
    for sc, a in scored:
        if len(kept) >= int(max_keep):
            break
        ok = True
        for _, b in kept:
            if b.size == a.size:
                d = a - b
                d2 = float(np.dot(d, d))
                if d2 < 0.0:
                    d2 = 0.0
                if math.sqrt(d2) < 0.03:
                    ok = False
                    break
        if ok:
            kept.append((sc, a))
    if not kept and scored:
        kept.append(scored[0])
    return kept


def _spectral_solver(start, target, n, m, iters=30, blend=0.5, score_every=10):
    n = int(max(8, n))
    L = 2 * n - 1
    t = np.asarray(target, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if t.size < L:
        tt = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        tt[:t.size] = t
        t = tt
    else:
        t = t[:L].astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    t = np.maximum(t, 0.0)
    ts = float(t.sum())
    if ts <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ts):
        t = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        ts = float(L)
    t /= ts

    a = _prep_sequence(start, n)
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-14)
    a = a / float(a.sum())

    T = np.fft.fft(t, m)
    sp = np.fft.ifft(np.sqrt(T)).real[:n]
    sp = np.maximum(sp, 0.0)
    sps = float(sp.sum())
    if sps <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sps):
        sp = a.copy()
    else:
        sp = sp / sps
    a = 0.6 * a + 0.4 * sp
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-14)
    a = a / float(a.sum())

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_fft(a)

    for it in range(int(max(1, iters))):
        F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(F)):
            break
        fmax = float(np.max(np.abs(F)))
        eps = 1e-10 * (fmax + 1.0)
        Fnew = 0.5 * (F + T / (F + eps))
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(Fnew)):
            break
        anew = np.fft.ifft(Fnew).real[:n]
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(anew)):
            break
        pos = np.maximum(anew, 0.0)
        ps = float(pos.sum())
        if ps < 1e-8 or not np.isfinite(ps):
            pos = a.copy()
        bld = max(0.2, float(blend) * (1.0 - 0.6 * float(it) / float(max(1, iters))))
        an = (1.0 - bld) * a + bld * pos
        an = np.maximum(an, 1e-14)
        ss = float(an.sum())
        if ss <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
            an = a.copy()
        else:
            an = an / ss
        a = an
        if (it + 1) % max(1, int(score_every)) == 0:
            sc = _score_fft(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best = a.copy()

    sc = _score_fft(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = sc
        best = a.copy()

    out = _prep_sequence(best, n)
    if out is None:
        return None, np.inf
    return out, float(best_sc)


def _spectral_starts(n, seed_a, budget):
    n = int(max(8, n))
    out = []
    if budget < 10.0:
        return out
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    xx = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L)

    targets = []
    targets.append(np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64))
    for eb in (0.3, 0.8):
        edge = 2.0 * np.abs(xx - 0.5)
        targets.append(1.0 + float(eb) * np.power(edge, 1.5))
        targets.append(1.0 + float(eb) * np.power(1.0 - edge, 1.5))

    starts = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)]
    if seed_a is not None:
        b = _prep_sequence(seed_a, n)
        if b is not None:
            starts.append(b)
            C = _conv_coeffs(b)
            cm = float(C.max())
            if cm > 0.0 and np.isfinite(cm):
                Cn = C / cm
                targets.append(Cn)
                targets.append(0.5 * Cn + 0.5 * np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64))

    iters = 25 if n > 4096 else 35
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    max_seconds = max(5.0, float(budget))
    for st in starts[:2]:
        for tg in targets[:4]:
            if time.monotonic() > t0 + 0.95 * max_seconds:
                break
            a, sc = _spectral_solver(st, tg, n, m, iters=iters, blend=0.5, score_every=8)
            if a is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
                out.append((sc, a))
    return out


def _local_simple(a, n):
    b = _prep_sequence(a, n)
    if b is None:
        return None, np.inf
    best = b.copy()
    best_s = _score_fft(b)

    sym = 0.5 * (b + b[::-1])
    b2 = _prep_sequence(sym)
    if b2 is not None:
        sc = _score_fft(b2)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-12:
            best = b2
            best_s = sc

    for r in (1, 2, 4, 8):
        k = np.ones(2 * r + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        k /= float(k.sum())
        sm = np.convolve(b, k, mode="same")
        b3 = _prep_sequence(sm)
        if b3 is not None:
            sc = _score_fft(b3)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-12:
                best = b3
                best_s = sc

    return best, float(best_s)


def _adam_polish(a, n, budget, rng):
    n = int(max(8, n))
    a, bs = _local_simple(a, n)
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        bs = _score_fft(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = float(bs)
    x = _x_from_a(a, n)
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    mu = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    nu = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, float(budget))
    last_improve = start

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        elapsed = max(1e-9, time.monotonic() - start)
        progress = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1.0, float(budget)))

        cm = _bmax(a)
        if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0.0:
            break
        frac = 0.25 * (1.0 - progress) + 0.01
        tau = max(1e-9, frac * cm)

        v = _v_from_x(x, n)
        if not np.isfinite(float(np.sum(v))):
            x += rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n - 1)
            continue
        aa = _softmax(v)
        pair = _score_grad_smooth(aa, tau, m)
        if not np.isfinite(pair[0]) or pair[1] is None:
            break
        g_a = pair[1]
        muv = float(np.dot(aa, g_a))
        gv = aa * (g_a - muv)
        gx = gv[:-1]
        if not np.isfinite(float(np.sum(gx))):
            x += rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n - 1)
            continue

        t += 1
        beta1 = 0.9
        beta2 = 0.999
        mu = beta1 * mu + (1.0 - beta1) * gx
        nu = beta2 * nu + (1.0 - beta2) * (gx * gx)
        b1 = beta1 ** t
        b2 = beta2 ** t
        mh = mu / max(1e-12, 1.0 - b1)
        vh = nu / max(1e-12, 1.0 - b2)
        lr = 0.30 * (1.0 - 0.7 * progress) + 0.02
        step = mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + 1e-8)
        step = np.clip(step, -0.25, 0.25)
        x = x - lr * step
        x = np.clip(x, -100.0, 100.0)

        if t % 10 == 0:
            newa = _a_from_x(x, n)
            newa, sc = _local_simple(newa, n)
            if newa is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
                a = newa
                if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    last_improve = time.monotonic()

        if time.monotonic() - last_improve > 30.0:
            sig = 0.04 + 0.06 * (1.0 - progress)
            x = x + rng.normal(0.0, sig, n - 1)
            x = np.clip(x, -100.0, 100.0)
            last_improve = time.monotonic()

    final = _local_simple(best_a, n)
    if final[0] is not None and final[1] < best_score:
        best_a = final[0]
        best_score = final[1]
    return best_a, float(best_score)


def _polish(a, n, budget, rng):
    n = int(max(8, n))
    a, bs = _local_simple(a, n)
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        bs = _score_fft(a)
    if _sciopt is None:
        return _adam_polish(a, n, max(1.0, float(budget)), rng)
    if budget < 20.0:
        a, bs = _local_simple(a, n)
        return a, float(bs)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = float(bs)
    x = _x_from_a(a, n)
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    bounds = [(-100.0, 100.0)] * max(1, n - 1)
    fracs = [1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125, 0.015, 0.0075, 0.00375, 0.0019]

    if n <= 2048:
        maxfun = 700
    elif n <= 4096:
        maxfun = 350
    else:
        maxfun = 150

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, float(budget))
    stage = 0
    no_improve = 0
    perturb_count = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0 and no_improve < 20:
        if stage < len(fracs):
            fr = fracs[stage]
        else:
            fr = max(0.001, fracs[-1] * (0.75 ** (stage - len(fracs))))

        cm = _bmax(a)
        if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0.0:
            break
        tau = max(1e-9, fr * cm)

        opts = {
            "maxiter": max(50, int(maxfun)),
            "maxfun": max(50, int(maxfun)),
            "ftol": 1e-15,
            "gtol": 1e-12,
            "disp": False,
        }

        try:
            res = _sciopt.minimize(
                _obj_x,
                x,
                jac=_grad_x,
                args=(n, tau, m),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options=opts,
            )
            if res is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                x = np.clip(res.x, -100.0, 100.0)
        except Exception as ex:
            print("L-BFGS-B failed: %s" % ex)

        a2 = _a_from_x(x, n)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a2)):
            break
        sc = _score_fft(a2)
        improved = False
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a2.copy()
            improved = True
            no_improve = 0
            print("  polish n=%d stage=%d fr=%.5f score=%.8f" % (n, stage, fr, sc))

        a2, sc2 = _local_simple(a2, n)
        if a2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc2
            best_a = a2.copy()
            if not improved:
                no_improve = 0
            print("  local n=%d stage=%d score=%.8f" % (n, stage, best_score))

        a = a2
        x = _x_from_a(a, n)
        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve >= 4:
            sig = 0.03 + 0.03 * math.sqrt(float(no_improve))
            x = x + rng.normal(0.0, sig, n - 1)
            x = np.clip(x, -100.0, 100.0)
            a = _a_from_x(x, n)
            no_improve = 0
            perturb_count += 1
            if perturb_count > 14:
                break

        stage += 1
        if stage > 120:
            break

    final = _local_simple(best_a, n)
    if final[0] is not None and final[1] < best_score:
        best_a = final[0]
        best_score = final[1]
    return best_a, float(best_score)


def _stage_search(n, budget, parent_seed, rng):
    n = int(max(8, n))
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(30.0, float(budget))
    cap = 320 if n <= 4096 else 220

    cands = _make_candidates(n, rng, parent_seed, cap)
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0 and n <= 8192:
        spec_budget = min(80.0, 0.25 * (deadline - time.monotonic()))
        spec = _spectral_starts(n, parent_seed, spec_budget)
        for _, a in spec:
            _add_candidate(cands, a)

    max_keep = 5 if n <= 2048 else (4 if n <= 4096 else 3)
    top = _select_top(cands, max_keep)
    if not top:
        ones = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        top = [(_score_fft(ones), ones)]

    best_a = top[0][1].copy()
    best_s = float(top[0][0])

    weights = [0.45, 0.25, 0.15, 0.10, 0.05][:len(top)]
    remw = float(sum(weights))

    for idx, (_, a) in enumerate(top):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 25.0:
            break
        w = float(weights[idx])
        alloc = rem * (w / remw if remw > 0.0 else 1.0)
        if idx == len(top) - 1:
            alloc = max(20.0, rem - 5.0)
        else:
            alloc = max(20.0, alloc)
        alloc = min(alloc, rem - 5.0)
        if alloc < 10.0:
            break
        print("stage n=%d opt cand=%d init=%.8f budget=%.1f" % (n, idx, top[idx][0], alloc))
        aa, ss = _polish(a, n, alloc, rng)
        if aa is not None and np.isfinite(ss) and ss < best_s:
            best_s = ss
            best_a = aa.copy()
        remw -= w

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 25.0:
        extra = rem - 5.0
        if n <= 2048:
            extra = min(350.0, extra)
        elif n <= 4096:
            extra = min(220.0, extra)
        else:
            extra = min(120.0, extra)
        if extra > 10.0:
            print("stage n=%d final polish budget=%.1f" % (n, extra))
            aa, ss = _polish(best_a, n, extra, rng)
            if aa is not None and np.isfinite(ss) and ss < best_s:
                best_s = ss
                best_a = aa.copy()

    final = _local_simple(best_a, n)
    if final[0] is not None and final[1] < best_s:
        best_a = final[0]
        best_s = final[1]
    return best_a, float(best_s)


def _add_unique(pool, a, thr=1e-4):
    b = _prep_sequence(a)
    if b is None or b.size == 0:
        return
    for e in pool:
        if e.size == b.size:
            d = b - e
            d2 = float(np.dot(d, d))
            if d2 < 0.0:
                d2 = 0.0
            if math.sqrt(d2) < advice and math.sqrt(d2) < float(thr):
                return
    pool.append(b)


advice = 0.0


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Main search routine invoked by the runner."""
    seed = int(seed)
    total_budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    start = time.monotonic()
    global_deadline = start + total_budget - 5.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        cand = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if cand is not None:
            p = _prep_sequence(cand)
            if p is not None and p.size > 0:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best_a = parent
    best_score = np.inf
    if parent is not None:
        best_score = _score_fft(parent)
        try:
            exact = evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in parent])
            if np.isfinite(exact):
                best_score = min(best_score, float(exact))
        except Exception:
            pass
        print("initial parent n=%d score=%.8f" % (parent.size, best_score))

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = _score_fft(best_a)

    history = [best_a.copy()]

    base = max(60.0, total_budget - 60.0)
    if parent is not None:
        specs = [(2048, 0.40), (4096, 0.34), (8192, 0.26)]
    else:
        specs = [(1024, 0.10), (2048, 0.35), (4096, 0.30), (8192, 0.25)]

    for n, frac in specs:
        now = time.monotonic()
        alloc = base * float(frac)
        if now + alloc > global_deadline:
            alloc = max(0.0, global_deadline - now - 10.0)
        if alloc < 45.0:
            continue
        print("===== stage n=%d budget=%.1fs current_best=%.8f =====" % (n, alloc, best_score))
        a_new, s_new = _stage_search(n, alloc, best_a, rng)
        if a_new is not None and np.isfinite(s_new) and s_new < best_score:
            best_score = float(s_new)
            best_a = a_new
            print("stage n=%d improved to %.8f" % (n, best_score))
            history.append(best_a.copy())
        elif a_new is not None and np.isfinite(s_new):
            history.append(a_new)

    final_pool_raw = []
    if best_a is not None:
        final_pool_raw.append(best_a)
    if parent is not None:
        final_pool_raw.append(parent)
    for h in history:
        if h is not None:
            final_pool_raw.append(h)

    final_pool = []
    for a in final_pool_raw:
        b = _prep_sequence(a)
        if b is None:
            continue
        _add_unique(final_pool, b, 1e-4)
        _add_unique(final_pool, 0.5 * (b + b[::-1]), 1e-4)
        for r in (1, 2, 4):
            k = np.ones(2 * r + 1, dtype=np.float64)
            k /= float(k.sum())
            sm = np.convolve(b, k, mode="same")
            _add_unique(final_pool, sm, 1e-4)

    scored_final = []
    for b in final_pool:
        sc = _score_fft(b)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored_final.append((sc, b))
    scored_final.sort(key=_pair_score)
    scored_final = scored_final[:12]

    exact_best = np.inf
    exact_seq = None
    count8192 = 0
    final_deadline = start + total_budget - 1.0

    for sc_fft, a in scored_final:
        n = int(a.size)
        if n > 8192:
            continue
        if n == 8192:
            count8192 += 1
            if count8192 > 2:
                continue
        if time.monotonic() > final_deadline - 0.5:
            break
        try:
            lst = [float(v) for v in a]
            ex = evaluate_sequence(lst)
            if np.isfinite(ex):
                ex = float(ex)
                if ex < exact_best:
                    exact_best = ex
                    exact_seq = a.copy()
                print("final exact n=%d exact=%.8f fft=%.8f" % (n, ex, sc_fft))
        except Exception as exerr:
            print("final exact failed: %s" % exerr)
            break

    if exact_seq is not None and exact_best < best_score:
        best_a = exact_seq
        best_score = exact_best

    final = _prep_sequence(best_a)
    if final is None:
        final = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)
    final = np.maximum(final, 0.0)
    print("returning final n=%d best_tracked=%.8f" % (final.size, best_score))
    return [float(v) for v in final]
```
